Abstract: While numerous machine unlearning (MU) methods have recently been developed with promising results in erasing the influence of forgotten data, classes, or concepts, they are also highly vulnerable-for example, simple fine-tuning can inadvertently reintroduce erased concepts. In this paper, we address this contradiction by examining the internal representations of unlearned models, in contrast to prior work that focuses primarily on output-level behavior. Our analysis shows that many state-of-the-art MU methods appear successful mainly due to a misalignment between last-layer features and the classifier, a phenomenon we call feature-classifier misalignment. In fact, hidden features remain highly discriminative, and simple linear probing can recover near-original accuracy. Assuming neural collapse in the original model, we further demonstrate that adjusting only the classifier can achieve negligible forget accuracy while preserving retain accuracy, and we corroborate this with experiments using classifier-only fine-tuning. Motivated by these findings, we propose MU methods based on a class-mean features (CMF) classifier, which explicitly enforces alignment between features and classifiers. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that CMF-based unlearning reduces forgotten information in representations while maintaining high retain accuracy, highlighting the need for faithful representation-level evaluation of MU.

</details>


### [152] [Dead Weights, Live Signals: Feedforward Graphs of Frozen Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08335)
*Marcus Armstrong,Navid Ayoobi,Arjun Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于冻结大语言模型的图架构，通过共享潜空间和线性投影实现异构模型间的通信，仅训练少量参数即可显著提升推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将多个异构的冻结大语言模型组合成计算图，利用它们各自的特长，通过共享潜空间进行协同推理，同时保持大部分参数冻结以降低计算成本

Method: 构建前馈图架构，其中异构的冻结LLM作为计算节点，通过学习的线性投影在共享连续潜空间中进行通信。三个小型冻结模型编码输入到共享潜空间，聚合信号注入两个大型冻结模型，最终通过轻量级交叉注意力输出节点生成结果。仅训练投影矩阵（17.6M参数）而保持约12B参数冻结

Result: 在ARC-Challenge上达到87.3%，OpenBookQA上82.8%，MMLU上67.2%，分别比最佳单个组成模型提升11.4、6.2和1.2个百分点，比参数匹配的冻结单模型分类器提升9.1、5.2和6.7个百分点。验证了梯度通过多个冻结模型边界的可行性，输出节点发展出选择性路由行为

Conclusion: 通过共享潜空间连接异构冻结LLM的图架构是有效的，仅训练少量参数即可显著提升推理性能，为构建高效的多模型系统提供了新思路

Abstract: We present a feedforward graph architecture in which heterogeneous frozen large language models serve as computational nodes, communicating through a shared continuous latent space via learned linear projections. Building on recent work demonstrating geometric compatibility between independently trained LLM latent spaces~\cite{armstrong2026thinking}, we extend this finding from static two-model steering to end-to-end trainable multi-node graphs, where projection matrices are optimized jointly via backpropagation through residual stream injection hooks. Three small frozen models (Llama-3.2-1B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, Gemma-2-2B) encode the input into a shared latent space whose aggregate signal is injected into two larger frozen models (Phi-3-mini, Mistral-7B), whose representations feed a lightweight cross-attention output node. With only 17.6M trainable parameters against approximately 12B frozen, the architecture achieves 87.3\% on ARC-Challenge, 82.8\% on OpenBookQA, and 67.2\% on MMLU, outperforming the best single constituent model by 11.4, 6.2, and 1.2 percentage points respectively, and outperforming parameter-matched learned classifiers on frozen single models by 9.1, 5.2, and 6.7 points. Gradient flow through multiple frozen model boundaries is empirically verified to be tractable, and the output node develops selective routing behavior across layer-2 nodes without explicit supervision.

</details>


### [153] [What Drives Representation Steering? A Mechanistic Case Study on Steering Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08524)
*Stephen Cheng,Sarah Wiegreffe,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过多令牌激活修补框架分析LLM中转向向量的因果机制，发现不同转向方法在相同层使用功能可互换的电路，转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，而忽略QK电路。转向向量可稀疏化90-99%仍保持性能，且不同转向方法在重要维度上具有一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然转向向量是大型语言模型对齐的高效技术，但缺乏对其工作原理的可解释性理解——具体来说，转向向量影响哪些内部机制以及如何导致不同的模型输出。为研究转向向量有效性的因果机制，本研究以拒绝行为为案例进行深入分析。

Method: 提出多令牌激活修补框架，对拒绝行为进行综合案例研究。通过数学分解转向OV电路，分析转向向量与注意力机制的交互方式。研究转向向量在不同模型家族中的表现，并探索转向向量的稀疏化可能性。

Result: 发现不同转向方法在相同层使用功能可互换的电路；转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，而忽略QK电路（冻结所有注意力分数仅使性能下降8.75%）；转向OV电路的数学分解揭示了语义可解释的概念；转向向量可稀疏化90-99%仍保持大部分性能；不同转向方法在重要维度上具有一致性。

Conclusion: 转向向量通过影响注意力机制中的OV电路而非QK电路来改变模型行为，这一机制具有功能可互换性和可解释性。转向向量的高度稀疏性表明其作用集中在特定维度，不同转向方法在核心机制上具有一致性，为模型对齐提供了更高效和可解释的技术路径。

Abstract: Applying steering vectors to large language models (LLMs) is an efficient and effective model alignment technique, but we lack an interpretable explanation for how it works-- specifically, what internal mechanisms steering vectors affect and how this results in different model outputs. To investigate the causal mechanisms underlying the effectiveness of steering vectors, we conduct a comprehensive case study on refusal. We propose a multi-token activation patching framework and discover that different steering methodologies leverage functionally interchangeable circuits when applied at the same layer. These circuits reveal that steering vectors primarily interact with the attention mechanism through the OV circuit while largely ignoring the QK circuit-- freezing all attention scores during steering drops performance by only 8.75% across two model families. A mathematical decomposition of the steered OV circuit further reveals semantically interpretable concepts, even in cases where the steering vector itself does not. Leveraging the activation patching results, we show that steering vectors can be sparsified by up to 90-99% while retaining most performance, and that different steering methodologies agree on a subset of important dimensions.

</details>


### [154] [Leveraging Complementary Embeddings for Replay Selection in Continual Learning with Small Buffers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08336)
*Danit Yanowsky,Daphna Weinshall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MERS：一种基于多嵌入重放选择的方法，通过整合监督和自监督嵌入来改进持续学习中的样本选择策略，在内存受限条件下显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中的灾难性遗忘是关键挑战，现有重放方法主要使用监督学习得到的嵌入构建记忆缓冲区，忽略了自监督表示中丰富的类别相关语义信息

Method: 提出多嵌入重放选择方法，用基于图的方法替换缓冲区选择模块，整合监督和自监督嵌入，无需增加模型参数或重放量

Result: 在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet上，MERS优于单嵌入基线方法，在低内存条件下表现尤为突出，为基于重放的持续学习提供了实用的即插即用增强

Conclusion: MERS通过整合监督和自监督嵌入，有效改进了持续学习中的样本选择策略，在内存受限条件下显著提升性能，是一种实用的增强方法

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a key challenge in Continual Learning (CL). In replay-based CL with severe memory constraints, performance critically depends on the sample selection strategy for the replay buffer. Most existing approaches construct memory buffers using embeddings learned under supervised objectives. However, class-agnostic, self-supervised representations often encode rich, class-relevant semantics that are overlooked. We propose a new method, Multiple Embedding Replay Selection, MERS, which replaces the buffer selection module with a graph-based approach that integrates both supervised and self-supervised embeddings. Empirical results show consistent improvements over SOTA selection strategies across a range of continual learning algorithms, with particularly strong gains in low-memory regimes. On CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet, MERS outperforms single-embedding baselines without adding model parameters or increasing replay volume, making it a practical, drop-in enhancement for replay-based continual learning.

</details>


### [155] [EgoEverything: A Benchmark for Human Behavior Inspired Long Context Egocentric Video Understanding in AR Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08342)
*Qiance Tang,Ziqi Wang,Jieyu Lin,Ziyun Li,Barbara De Salvo,Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EgoEverything是一个用于AR长上下文第一人称视频理解的基准测试，通过整合人类注意力信号生成问题，包含5000多个选择题对，覆盖100多小时视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称视频数据集主要依赖人类佩戴的摄像头，侧重于视觉内容，对用户行为考虑有限，缺乏真实的人类注意力信号来指导问题生成，难以准确评估AR环境下的长上下文视频理解能力。

Method: 利用从注视数据中抽象出的人类注意力信号来生成问题，构建包含5000多个多项选择题对的数据集，覆盖超过100小时的视频内容，通过整合人类注意力信号来更真实地模拟自然人类行为。

Result: 创建了EgoEverything基准测试，包含5000多个多项选择题对，涵盖100多小时的第一人称视频，通过整合人类注意力信号提供了更真实的AR长上下文视频理解评估环境。

Conclusion: EgoEverything通过整合人类注意力信号生成问题，为AR应用中的长上下文第一人称视频理解提供了更真实、更符合人类行为的评估基准，解决了现有数据集在模拟真实用户行为方面的局限性。

Abstract: Long context egocentric video understanding has recently attracted significant research attention, with augmented reality (AR) highlighted as one of its most important application domains. Nevertheless, the task remains highly challenging due to the need for reasoning over extended temporal contexts and diverse, unstructured activities. Although several benchmarks exist, most egocentric datasets rely on human worn cameras and focus mainly on visual content, with limited consideration of underlying user behavior when forming video-related queries. EgoEverything is a benchmark that explicitly considers human behavior by leveraging human attention signals, abstracted from gaze data, when generating questions. It comprises over 5,000 multiple choice question answer pairs, spanning more than 100 hours of video. By integrating human attention signals during question generation, it more faithfully captures natural human behavior and offers a realistic evaluation setting for long-context egocentric video understanding in AR.

</details>


### [156] [Scaling-Aware Data Selection for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08366)
*Tolga Dimlioglu,Nadine Chang,Maying Shen,Rafid Mahmood,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个用于优化训练数据选择的数据选择框架，通过分区、拟合神经缩放定律和迭代优化数据混合来提升模型性能，在自动驾驶场景中比基线方法用少80%的数据获得更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模深度学习模型需要多样化的训练数据，但现有数据选择框架无法处理数据点对不同评估指标影响的模糊性，需要一种能考虑多指标优化且高效的数据选择方法。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，包含三个步骤：1) 将数据集划分为不同域；2) 为每个数据域拟合神经缩放定律到评估指标；3) 通过迭代添加能最大化指标变化的数据域来优化数据混合。

Result: 在自动驾驶端到端规划器模型上应用MOSAIC，使用扩展预测驾驶员模型评分(EPDMS)作为评估指标，MOSAIC在EPDMS上优于多种基线方法，且数据使用量减少高达80%。

Conclusion: MOSAIC提供了一个通用的数据选择框架，能够有效优化训练数据混合，在保持或提升模型性能的同时显著减少所需数据量，特别适用于需要多指标评估的物理AI应用。

Abstract: Large-scale deep learning models for physical AI applications depend on diverse training data collection efforts. These models and correspondingly, the training data, must address different evaluation criteria necessary for the models to be deployable in real-world environments. Data selection policies can guide the development of the training set, but current frameworks do not account for the ambiguity in how data points affect different metrics. In this work, we propose Mixture Optimization via Scaling-Aware Iterative Collection (MOSAIC), a general data selection framework that operates by: (i) partitioning the dataset into domains; (ii) fitting neural scaling laws from each data domain to the evaluation metrics; and (iii) optimizing a data mixture by iteratively adding data from domains that maximize the change in metrics. We apply MOSAIC to autonomous driving (AD), where an End-to-End (E2E) planner model is evaluated on the Extended Predictive Driver Model Score (EPDMS), an aggregate of driving rule compliance metrics. Here, MOSAIC outperforms a diverse set of baselines on EPDMS with up to 80\% less data.

</details>


### [157] [ADAPTive Input Training for Many-to-One Pre-Training on Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08398)
*Paul Quinlan,Qingguo Li,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADAPT提出了一种新的时间序列预训练范式，能够有效对齐时间序列数据的物理特性，支持在输入尺寸和通道维度差异极大的数据集上进行混合批次预训练，为构建时间序列基础模型奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列自监督预训练方法在单一数据集预训练、下游数据集微调的一对多场景中表现良好，但在预训练阶段加入更多数据集时难以泛化到新数据集。这限制了利用大量多样化数据集构建时间序列基础模型的能力。

Method: ADAPT是一种新的时间序列预训练范式，能够高效对齐时间序列数据的物理特性，即使在预训练数据的输入尺寸和通道维度存在极端差异的情况下，也能实现混合批次预训练。

Result: 在162个时间序列分类数据集上进行训练，在分类基准测试中取得了新的最先进性能。成功在时间序列领域同时训练了一个模型，覆盖了广泛的数据集范围。

Conclusion: ADAPT是构建时间序列领域通用基础模型的重要构建模块，解决了现有预训练方法在多数据集预训练时泛化能力不足的问题。

Abstract: Recent work on time-series models has leveraged self-supervised training to learn meaningful features and patterns in order to improve performance on downstream tasks and generalize to unseen modalities. While these pretraining methods have shown great promise in one-to-many scenarios, where a model is pre-trained on one dataset and fine-tuned on a downstream dataset, they have struggled to generalize to new datasets when more datasets are added during pre-training. This is a fundamental challenge in building foundation models for time-series data, as it limits the ability to develop models that can learn from a large variety of diverse datasets available. To address this challenge, we present a new pre-training paradigm for time-series data called ADAPT, which can efficiently align the physical properties of data in the time-series domain, enabling mixed-batch pre-training despite the extreme discrepancies in the input sizes and channel dimensions of pre-training data. We trained on 162 time-series classification datasets and set new state-of-the-art performance for classification benchmarks. We successfully train a model within the time-series domain on a wide range of datasets simultaneously, which is a major building block for building generalist foundation models in time-series domains.

</details>


### [158] [Zero-shot Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Using Tabular Prior Fitted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08400)
*Mayuka Jayawardhana,Nihal Sharma,Kazem Meidani,Bayan Bruss,Tom Goldstein,Doron Bergman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-TS：一种将多元时间序列预测重新构造为标量回归问题，利用表格基础模型进行零样本预测的通用框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（如TabPFN）在表格数据任务中表现出色，但应用于时间序列预测时，大多将多元时间序列分解为多个独立的单变量预测子问题，忽略了通道间的相互作用。需要克服这一限制，开发能够处理多元时间序列交互的通用框架。

Method: 提出一个通用框架，将多元时间序列预测问题重新构造为一系列标量回归问题。该框架可将任何具有回归能力的表格基础模型应用于时间序列预测，实现零样本学习。具体使用TabPFN-TS作为骨干模型。

Result: 使用TabPFN-TS骨干模型的方法在性能上与当前最先进的表格方法进行了比较，展示了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够克服现有方法忽略多元时间序列通道间相互作用的限制，为表格基础模型在多元时间序列预测中的应用提供了通用解决方案，实现了零样本预测能力。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models, particularly Prior-data Fitted Networks like TabPFN have emerged as the leading contender in a myriad of tasks ranging from data imputation to label prediction on the tabular data format surpassing the historical successes of tree-based models. This has led to investigations on their applicability to forecasting time series data which can be formulated as a tabular problem. While recent work to this end has displayed positive results, most works have limited their treatment of multivariate time series problems to several independent univariate time series forecasting subproblems, thus ignoring any inter-channel interactions. Overcoming this limitation, we introduce a generally applicable framework for multivariate time series forecasting using tabular foundation models. We achieve this by recasting the multivariate time series forecasting problem as a series of scalar regression problems which can then be solved zero-shot by any tabular foundation model with regression capabilities. We present results of our method using the TabPFN-TS backbone and compare performance with the current state of the art tabular methods.

</details>


### [159] [Adversarial Label Invariant Graph Data Augmentations for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08404)
*Simon Zhang,Ryan P. DeMilt,Kun Jin,Cathy H. Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RIA是一种用于协变量偏移下OoD泛化的新方法，通过对抗性训练实现不变性正则化，基于Q学习类比，使用对抗性标签不变数据增强来探索训练数据环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实应用中，训练和测试数据来自不同环境时会出现分布偏移。协变量偏移是分布偏移的一种类型，仅发生在输入数据中，而概念分布保持不变。现有方法需要改进以更好地处理这种OoD泛化问题。

Method: 提出RIA方法，通过对抗性标签不变数据增强创建新环境，防止模型坍缩为仅适应训练分布的学习器。该方法可与许多现有的OoD泛化方法结合，采用交替梯度下降-上升算法求解约束优化问题。

Result: 在OoD图分类任务上进行了大量实验，涵盖各种合成和自然分布偏移。实验表明，与现有OoD基线方法相比，RIA能够实现更高的准确率。

Conclusion: RIA是一种有效的OoD泛化方法，特别适用于协变量偏移场景，通过对抗性训练探索数据环境，提高了模型在分布偏移下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OoD) generalization occurs when representation learning encounters a distribution shift. This occurs frequently in practice when training and testing data come from different environments. Covariate shift is a type of distribution shift that occurs only in the input data, while the concept distribution stays invariant. We propose RIA - Regularization for Invariance with Adversarial training, a new method for OoD generalization under convariate shift. Motivated by an analogy to $Q$-learning, it performs an adversarial exploration for training data environments. These new environments are induced by adversarial label invariant data augmentations that prevent a collapse to an in-distribution trained learner. It works with many existing OoD generalization methods for covariate shift that can be formulated as constrained optimization problems. We develop an alternating gradient descent-ascent algorithm to solve the problem, and perform extensive experiments on OoD graph classification for various kinds of synthetic and natural distribution shifts. We demonstrate that our method can achieve high accuracy compared with OoD baselines.

</details>


### [160] [Provably Adaptive Linear Approximation for the Shapley Value and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08438)
*Weida Li,Yaoliang Yu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Adalina算法，在Θ(n)空间约束下高效近似半值，实现线性时间、线性空间复杂度，改进了均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shapley值及其半值家族在归因问题中应用广泛，但精确计算需要指数级效用查询。大规模应用需要高效近似算法，特别是在Θ(n)空间约束下。

Method: 基于向量集中不等式建立理论框架，统一OFA、unbiased kernelSHAP、SHAP-IQ和回归调整方法。开发线性空间算法，需要O(n/ε² log(1/δ))次查询。提出自适应线性时间线性空间随机算法Adalina，显式最小化每个效用函数的均方误差。

Result: 理论框架为现有无偏随机算法提供更严格的查询复杂度分析。Adalina算法在理论上实现改进的均方误差，所有理论发现均通过实验验证。

Conclusion: 建立了半值高效近似的理论框架，统一了多种现有方法，提出了首个自适应线性时间线性空间随机算法Adalina，在Θ(n)空间约束下实现了改进的近似性能。

Abstract: The Shapley value, and its broader family of semi-values, has received much attention in various attribution problems. A fundamental and long-standing challenge is their efficient approximation, since exact computation generally requires an exponential number of utility queries in the number of players $n$. To meet the challenges of large-scale applications, we explore the limits of efficiently approximating semi-values under a $Θ(n)$ space constraint. Building upon a vector concentration inequality, we establish a theoretical framework that enables sharper query complexities for existing unbiased randomized algorithms. Within this framework, we systematically develop a linear-space algorithm that requires $O(\frac{n}{ε^{2}}\log\frac{1}δ)$ utility queries to ensure $P(\|\hat{\boldsymbolφ}-\boldsymbolφ\|_{2}\geqε)\leq δ$ for all commonly used semi-values. In particular, our framework naturally bridges OFA, unbiased kernelSHAP, SHAP-IQ and the regression-adjusted approach, and definitively characterizes when paired sampling is beneficial. Moreover, our algorithm allows explicit minimization of the mean square error for each specific utility function. Accordingly, we introduce the first adaptive, linear-time, linear-space randomized algorithm, Adalina, that theoretically achieves improved mean square error. All of our theoretical findings are experimentally validated.

</details>


### [161] [Less Approximates More: Harmonizing Performance and Confidence Faithfulness via Hybrid Post-Training for High-Stakes Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08454)
*Haokai Ma,Lee Yan Zhen,Gang Yang,Yunshan Ma,Ee-Chien Chang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyTuning：一种混合后训练框架，通过渐进推理增益（PRG）度量自适应重加权推理蒸馏（RD）和内部反馈强化学习（RLIF），在有限监督下提升准确性和置信度忠实性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险任务中部署时，过度自信但错误的推断可能导致严重现实危害，因此置信度忠实性问题变得至关重要。现有联合优化方法面临三大挑战：高质量训练语料稀缺、事实依据不足的过度自信、以及错误融合放大错误更新

Method: 提出渐进推理增益（PRG）度量推理步骤是否逐步强化对最终答案的支持；引入HyTuning混合后训练框架，通过PRG风格度量自适应重加权推理蒸馏（RD）和内部反馈强化学习（RLIF），使用稀缺监督推理轨迹作为稳定锚点，同时利用大量未标记查询实现可扩展性

Result: 在多个领域特定和通用基准测试中，HyTuning在有限监督下提高了准确性，同时实现了置信度忠实性，支持"更少近似更多"的实际效果

Conclusion: HyTuning通过渐进推理增益度量和自适应混合优化，有效解决了大语言模型置信度忠实性问题，在有限监督资源下实现了准确性和置信校准的平衡，为高风险应用提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes tasks, where confident yet incorrect inferences may cause severe real-world harm, bringing the previously overlooked issue of confidence faithfulness back to the forefront. A promising solution is to jointly optimize unsupervised Reinforcement Learning from Internal Feedback (RLIF) with reasoning-trace-guided Reasoning Distillation (RD), which may face three persistent challenges: scarcity of high-quality training corpora, factually unwarranted overconfidence and indiscriminate fusion that amplifies erroneous updates. Inspired by the human confidence accumulation from uncertainty to certainty, we propose Progressive Reasoning Gain (PRG) to measure whether reasoning steps progressively strengthen support for the final answer. Furthermore, we introduce HyTuning, a hybrid post-training framework that adaptively reweights RD and RLIF via a PRG-style metric, using scarce supervised reasoning traces as a stable anchor while exploiting abundant unlabeled queries for scalability. Experiments on several domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that HyTuning improves accuracy while achieving confidence faithfulness under limited supervision, supporting a practical "Less Approximates More" effect.

</details>


### [162] [A Machine Learning Framework for Turbofan Health Estimation via Inverse Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08460)
*Milad Leyli-Abadi,Lucas Thil,Sebastien Razakarivony,Guillaume Doquet,Jesse Read*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种从涡轮风扇发动机传感器数据中恢复部件级健康指标的新方法，引入了包含维护事件和工况变化等工业复杂性的新数据集，建立了稳态与非稳态数据驱动模型、贝叶斯滤波器的基准，并探索了自监督学习方法在无真实健康标签下的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮风扇发动机健康状态估计是一个具有挑战性的不适定逆问题，现有研究存在碎片化、使用不现实数据集、对时间信息利用不足等问题。研究旨在从运行传感器数据中恢复部件级健康指标，考虑实际退化模式和维护事件。

Method: 1) 引入包含工业复杂性（维护事件、使用变化）的新数据集；2) 建立稳态与非稳态数据驱动模型、贝叶斯滤波器的基准比较；3) 提出自监督学习方法，在无真实健康标签情况下学习潜在表示；4) 通过下游估计性能比较评估方法效果。

Result: 结果显示传统滤波器仍然是强有力的基准，自监督学习方法揭示了健康估计的内在复杂性，并突显了对更先进、可解释推理策略的需求。研究为问题难度建立了实际下界。

Conclusion: 该研究通过新数据集和系统基准测试，揭示了涡轮风扇发动机健康估计的挑战性，传统方法仍具竞争力，而自监督学习暴露了问题的本质复杂性，需要更先进、可解释的推理策略。数据集和实现代码已开源以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Estimating the health state of turbofan engines is a challenging ill-posed inverse problem, hindered by sparse sensing and complex nonlinear thermodynamics. Research in this area remains fragmented, with comparisons limited by the use of unrealistic datasets and insufficient exploration of the exploitation of temporal information. This work investigates how to recover component-level health indicators from operational sensor data under realistic degradation and maintenance patterns. To support this study, we introduce a new dataset that incorporates industry-oriented complexities such as maintenance events and usage changes. Using this dataset, we establish an initial benchmark that compares steady-state and nonstationary data-driven models, and Bayesian filters, classic families of methods used to solve this problem. In addition to this benchmark, we introduce self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches that learn latent representations without access to true health labels, a scenario reflective of real-world operational constraints. By comparing the downstream estimation performance of these unsupervised representations against the direct prediction baselines, we establish a practical lower bound on the difficulty of solving this inverse problem. Our results reveal that traditional filters remain strong baselines, while SSL methods reveal the intrinsic complexity of health estimation and highlight the need for more advanced and interpretable inference strategies. For reproducibility, both the generated dataset and the implementation used in this work are made accessible.

</details>


### [163] [TTVS: Boosting Self-Exploring Reinforcement Learning via Test-time Variational Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08468)
*Sikai Bai,Haoxi Li,Jie Zhang,Yongjiang Liu,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTVS框架通过动态合成测试查询的语义等价变体，使大型推理模型能够在无标签测试数据上自我进化，克服了传统测试时方法依赖静态查询集导致的过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习范式在专业或新颖领域中面临监督成本过高或不可得的限制，而传统测试时方法因依赖静态查询集容易过拟合文本模式，无法有效适应测试环境。

Method: 提出测试时变分合成框架，包含两个协同模块：在线变分合成模块将静态测试查询转换为动态多样的语义等价变体流，迫使模型学习底层问题逻辑而非表面模式；测试时混合探索模块在合成变体间平衡准确性驱动的利用和一致性驱动的探索。

Result: 在八种模型架构上的广泛实验表明，TTVS仅使用无标签测试数据就超越了其他测试时适应方法，甚至优于基于大量高质量标签数据训练的监督强化学习技术。

Conclusion: TTVS为大型推理模型在监督稀缺或不可得的领域提供了有效的测试时自适应解决方案，通过动态合成训练流实现了从模式记忆到逻辑学习的转变。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) driven by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), this paradigm is fundamentally limited in specialized or novel domains where such supervision is prohibitively expensive or unavailable, posing a key challenge for test-time adaptation. While existing test-time methods offer a potential solution, they are constrained by learning from static query sets, risking overfitting to textual patterns. To address this gap, we introduce Test-Time Variational Synthesis (TTVS), a novel framework that enables LRMs to self-evolve by dynamically augmenting the training stream from unlabeled test queries. TTVS comprises two synergistic modules: (1) Online Variational Synthesis, which transforms static test queries into a dynamic stream of diverse, semantically-equivalent variations, enforcing the model to learn underlying problem logic rather than superficial patterns; (2) Test-time Hybrid Exploration, which balances accuracy-driven exploitation with consistency-driven exploration across synthetic variants. Extensive experiments show TTVS yields superior performance across eight model architectures. Notably, using only unlabeled test-time data, TTVS not only surpasses other test-time adaptation methods but also outperforms state-of-the-art supervised RL-based techniques trained on vast, high-quality labeled data.

</details>


### [164] [Quantization Impact on the Accuracy and Communication Efficiency Trade-off in Federated Learning for Aerospace Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08474)
*Abdelkarim Loukili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习中对称均匀量化对航空航天预测性维护的影响，发现INT4量化在保持与FP32统计上无差异精度的同时，将梯度通信成本降低8倍，而INT2量化则因过度正则化导致性能不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习（FL）能够在分布式航空航天机队中实现隐私保护的预测性维护，但梯度通信开销限制了其在带宽受限的物联网节点上的部署。需要研究量化技术对精度-效率权衡的影响。

Method: 使用对称均匀量化（b∈{32,8,4,2}位）对自定义设计的轻量级1-D卷积模型（AeroConv1D，9,697参数）进行联邦学习训练。在NASA C-MAPSS基准数据集上，采用现实的非独立同分布（Non-IID）客户端分区，进行严格的多种子评估（N=10种子）。通过IID与Non-IID分区的直接比较，揭示量化稳定性差异。

Result: INT4量化在FD001（p=0.341）和FD002（p=0.264 MAE，p=0.534 NASA评分）上达到与FP32统计上无差异的精度，同时将每轮梯度通信成本从37.88 KiB降低到4.73 KiB（8倍减少）。INT2量化在FD002上通过极端量化诱导的过度正则化获得较低MAE，但伴随灾难性的NASA评分不稳定性（CV=45.8% vs. FP32的22.3%）。Xilinx ZCU102上的FPGA资源分析显示INT4满足硬件约束（85.5% DSP利用率）。

Conclusion: INT4量化是航空航天联邦学习预测性维护的可行解决方案，在保持精度的同时显著降低通信开销。研究强调了正确评估非独立同分布客户端分区的重要性，揭示了极端量化（INT2）在实际操作条件下的不稳定性。INT4量化方案有望在单个SoC上实现完整的联邦学习流水线。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving predictive maintenance across distributed aerospace fleets, but gradient communication overhead constrains deployment on bandwidth-limited IoT nodes. This paper investigates the impact of symmetric uniform quantization ($b \in \{32,8,4,2\}$ bits) on the accuracy--efficiency trade-off of a custom-designed lightweight 1-D convolutional model (AeroConv1D, 9\,697 parameters) trained via FL on the NASA C-MAPSS benchmark under a realistic Non-IID client partition. Using a rigorous multi-seed evaluation ($N=10$ seeds), we show that INT4 achieves accuracy \emph{statistically indistinguishable} from FP32 on both FD001 ($p=0.341$) and FD002 ($p=0.264$ MAE, $p=0.534$ NASA score) while delivering an $8\times$ reduction in gradient communication cost (37.88~KiB $\to$ 4.73~KiB per round). A key methodological finding is that naïve IID client partitioning artificially suppresses variance; correct Non-IID evaluation reveals the true operational instability of extreme quantization, demonstrated via a direct empirical IID vs.\ Non-IID comparison. INT2 is empirically characterized as unsuitable: while it achieves lower MAE on FD002 through extreme quantization-induced over-regularization, this apparent gain is accompanied by catastrophic NASA score instability (CV\,=\,45.8\% vs.\ 22.3\% for FP32), confirming non-reproducibility under heterogeneous operating conditions. Analytical FPGA resource projections on the Xilinx ZCU102 confirm that INT4 fits within hardware constraints (85.5\% DSP utilization), potentially enabling a complete FL pipeline on a single SoC. The full simulation codebase and FPGA estimation scripts are publicly available at https://github.com/therealdeadbeef/aerospace-fl-quantization.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Linearly Solvable Continuous-Time General-Sum Stochastic Differential Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07479)
*Monika Tomar,Takashi Tanaka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出一种连续时间有限玩家随机微分博弈，通过精确线性PDE系统求解，利用交叉对数似然比建模多智能体空间冲突，通过广义多元Cole-Hopf变换将非线性HJB方程解耦为线性PDE系统，实现无网格反馈纳什均衡计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多智能体随机微分博弈中的维度灾难问题，传统方法难以高效计算反馈纳什均衡策略，特别是在处理空间冲突（如拥堵避免）等复杂交互场景时。

Method: 1. 构建基于交叉对数似然比的分布规划博弈框架，自然建模多智能体空间冲突；2. 应用广义多元Cole-Hopf变换，将非线性Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程解耦为线性偏微分方程系统；3. 利用Feynman-Kac路径积分方法进行无网格计算。

Result: 实现了反馈纳什均衡策略的高效计算，有效克服了维度灾难问题，能够处理连续时间有限玩家随机一般和微分博弈。

Conclusion: 该方法为多智能体随机微分博弈提供了一种新颖的解析框架，通过线性化变换和路径积分方法实现了复杂交互场景下的高效均衡计算。

Abstract: This paper introduces a class of continuous-time, finite-player stochastic general-sum differential games that admit solutions through an exact linear PDE system. We formulate a distribution planning game utilizing the cross-log-likelihood ratio to naturally model multi-agent spatial conflicts, such as congestion avoidance. By applying a generalized multivariate Cole-Hopf transformation, we decouple the associated non-linear Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equations into a system of linear partial differential equations. This reduction enables the efficient, grid-free computation of feedback Nash equilibrium strategies via the Feynman-Kac path integral method, effectively overcoming the curse of dimensionality.

</details>


### [166] [Parameter-free non-ergodic extragradient algorithms for solving monotone variational inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07662)
*Lingqing Shen,Fatma Kılınç-Karzan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出参数无关的外梯度方法，用于单调变分不等式问题，具有非渐近的最后迭代保证，无需全局光滑性信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外梯度方法严重依赖步长选择，通常需要问题特定的全局光滑性信息，而这些信息往往难以准确估计甚至不适用。同时，现有理论多关注迭代的遍历平均，而实际性能通常由最后迭代的更强行为驱动。

Method: 开发参数无关的外梯度方法：1) 对于全局Lipschitz算子，实现o(1/√T)的最后迭代收敛率；2) 通过回溯线搜索扩展到局部Lipschitz算子，保持参数无关性；3) 提供非渐近的最后迭代保证。

Result: 1) 对全局Lipschitz算子实现o(1/√T)的最后迭代收敛率；2) 对局部Lipschitz算子通过回溯线搜索获得相同收敛率；3) 在双线性矩阵博弈、LASSO、极小极大群体公平性和最大熵采样松弛等实验中表现出广泛适用性和优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的参数无关外梯度方法为约束单调变分不等式问题提供了有效的最后迭代保证，无需全局光滑性信息，适用于更广泛的问题类别，在实际应用中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Monotone variational inequalities (VIs) provide a unifying framework for convex minimization, equilibrium computation, and convex-concave saddle-point problems. Extragradient-type methods are among the most effective first-order algorithms for such problems, but their performance hinges critically on stepsize selection. While most existing theory focuses on ergodic averages of the iterates, practical performance is often driven by the significantly stronger behavior of the last iterate. Moreover, available last-iterate guarantees typically rely on fixed stepsizes chosen using problem-specific global smoothness information, which is often difficult to estimate accurately and may not even be applicable. In this paper, we develop parameter-free extragradient methods with non-asymptotic last-iterate guarantees for constrained monotone VIs. For globally Lipschitz operators, our algorithm achieves an $o(1/\sqrt{T})$ last-iterate rate. We then extend the framework to locally Lipschitz operators via backtracking line search and obtain the same rate while preserving parameter-freeness, thereby making parameter-free last-iterate methods applicable to important problem classes for which global smoothness is unrealistic. Our numerical experiments on bilinear matrix games, LASSO, minimax group fairness, and state-of-the-art maximum entropy sampling relaxations demonstrate wide applicability of our results as well as strong last-iterate performance and significant improvements over existing methods.

</details>


### [167] [On Linear Critical-Region Boundaries in Continuous-Time Multiparametric Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07693)
*Lida Lamakani,Efstratios N. Pistikopoulos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文证明了连续时间显式控制中临界区域边界为超平面的充要条件：当相关极值点出现在初始或终端时刻时，边界为超平面；当极值点随初始条件变化时，边界为曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究连续时间多参数显式控制中临界区域边界的几何特性，特别是区分超平面边界和曲面边界的条件，这对于构建有效的显式控制分区至关重要。

Method: 通过分析最优控制问题中拉格朗日乘子和约束函数的极值点特性，证明边界为超平面的充要条件是极值点出现在固定端点（初始或终端时刻）。利用共态变量是初始状态的线性函数这一特性，推导边界法向量的解析表达式。

Result: 建立了边界几何特性的判别准则：当极值点出现在固定端点时，边界为超平面且可通过矩阵指数和线性求解计算；当极值点随初始条件变化时，边界一般为曲面。在三阶系统上首次获得了完整的三维临界区域分区解析解。

Conclusion: 连续时间显式控制的分区边界特性由极值点的时间位置决定，这一发现为构建高效的多参数控制分区提供了理论基础，同时揭示了连续时间与离散时间方法在分区复杂度上的显著差异。

Abstract: When an optimal control problem is solved for all possible initial conditions at once, the initial-state space splits into critical regions, each carrying a closed-form control law that can be evaluated online without solving any optimization. This is the multiparametric approach to explicit control. In the continuous-time setting, the boundaries between these regions are determined by extrema of Lagrange multipliers and constraint functions along the optimal trajectory. Whether a boundary is a hyperplane, computable analytically, or a curved manifold that requires numerical methods has a direct effect on how the partition is built.
  We show that a boundary is a hyperplane if and only if the relevant extremum is attained at either the initial time or the terminal time, regardless of the initial condition. The reason is that the costate is a linear function of the initial state at any fixed time, so when the extremum is tied to a fixed endpoint, the boundary condition is linear and the boundary normal follows directly from two matrix exponentials and a linear solve. When the extremum occurs at a time that shifts with the initial condition, such as a switching time or an interior stationary point, the boundary is generally curved.
  We demonstrate the result on a third-order system, obtaining the complete three-dimensional critical-region partition analytically for the first time in this problem class. A comparison with a discrete-time formulation shows how sharply the region count grows under discretization, while the continuous-time partition remains unchanged.

</details>


### [168] [Optimizing Treatment Allocation to Maximize the Health of a Population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07738)
*Daniel Adelman,Alba V Olivares-Nadal,Miaolan Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于测度化MDP的PHM优化方法，通过有限患者集简化高维问题，生成可临床实施的指数策略，在CMS数据上显著优于短视基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PHM优化面临三大挑战：高维患者协变量管理、长期干预响应跟踪、人口流入流出动态。现有方法难以同时处理这些复杂因素，需要新框架来优化稀缺医疗资源分配。

Method: 采用测度化MDP框架，将问题建模为在容量约束下优化医疗人口长期分布。引入非伤害性约束限制死亡率，使用近似动态规划将无限维问题简化为识别有限的高性能治疗/未治疗患者集，最终推导出基于调整影响度的指数策略。

Result: 在CMS数据上验证，策略相比短视基准有统计显著改进，优势随时间范围增加而增强。最长测试时间范围内，每1000患者每年可增加超过1500个居家天数。

Conclusion: 测度化MDP框架有效解决了PHM优化中的高维和动态挑战，生成的简单指数策略既临床可实施又灵活，能整合机器学习模型，为稀缺医疗资源分配提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Recent shifts in global health priorities have positioned Population Health Management (PHM) as a central area of focus. However, optimizing PHM strategies presents several challenges: managing high-dimensional patient covariates, tracking their evolution and long-term response to interventions, and accounting for the inflow and outflow of individuals within the population. In this paper, we propose a novel approach based on Measurized MDPs that integrates these components. We consider a setting in which a treatment with population-level benefits is available but scarce, and model an MDP that optimizes the long-term distribution of the healthcare population under expected capacity constraints. This formulation allows us to bypass both the dimensionality and practical challenges of handling and tracking individual patient covariates across the population. To ensure ethical compliance, we introduce a non-maleficence constraint that limits the allowable mortality rate. To solve the resulting infinite-dimensional problem, we use ADP and reduce the task to identifying a finite set of high-performing treated and untreated patients. Despite the complexity of the underlying structure, our approach yields a simple, clinically implementable index policy: a patient is selected for treatment if their adjusted impactability exceeds a specified threshold. The adjusted impactability captures the long-term consequences of receiving or not receiving treatment. While straightforward to apply, the policy remains flexible and can incorporate general machine learning models. Using CMS data, we show that our policy yields a statistically significant improvement over a myopic benchmark. This advantage increases with the time horizon, consistent with the forward-looking nature of our policy. At the longest horizon tested, this corresponds to over 1,500 additional home days annually per 1,000 patients.

</details>


### [169] [Unified Precision-Guaranteed Stopping Rules for Contextual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07913)
*Mingrui Ding,Qiuhong Zhao,Siyang Gao,Jing Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 开发基于广义似然比统计量的统一停止规则，用于上下文学习中的个性化决策问题，在未知抽样方差下保证学习策略的精度，显著减少所需样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在运营管理中，上下文学习通过数据收集学习将个体特征映射到行动的决策策略。关键问题是如何确定何时可以停止数据收集，同时保证学习到的策略足够准确。现有方法在确定停止时机方面缺乏统一框架，且往往需要过多样本。

Method: 提出基于广义似然比(GLR)统计量的统一停止规则，用于上下文学习的两种精度标准：上下文标准和聚合策略价值标准。开发新的时间一致偏差不等式来校准顺序边界，直接控制自归一化GLR证据，避免因解耦均值和方差不确定性导致的保守性。在结构化线性设置和非结构化设置中均适用。

Result: 在高斯抽样模型下，为两种精度标准建立了有限样本精度保证。数值实验表明，所提停止规则在合成实例和两个案例研究中，相比基准方法显著减少所需样本量，同时达到目标精度。

Conclusion: 该框架为个性化决策问题提供了确定何时收集足够信息的实用方法，适用于多种数据收集环境（历史数据集、仿真模型、实际系统），使从业者能够在保持决策质量的同时减少不必要的抽样。

Abstract: Contextual learning seeks to learn a decision policy that maps an individual's characteristics to an action through data collection. In operations management, such data may come from various sources, and a central question is when data collection can stop while still guaranteeing that the learned policy is sufficiently accurate. We study this question under two precision criteria: a context-wise criterion and an aggregate policy-value criterion. We develop unified stopping rules for contextual learning with unknown sampling variances in both unstructured and structured linear settings. Our approach is based on generalized likelihood ratio (GLR) statistics for pairwise action comparisons. To calibrate the corresponding sequential boundaries, we derive new time-uniform deviation inequalities that directly control the self-normalized GLR evidence and thus avoid the conservativeness caused by decoupling mean and variance uncertainty. Under the Gaussian sampling model, we establish finite-sample precision guarantees for both criteria. Numerical experiments on synthetic instances and two case studies demonstrate that the proposed stopping rules achieve the target precision with substantially fewer samples than benchmark methods. The proposed framework provides a practical way to determine when enough information has been collected in personalized decision problems. It applies across multiple data-collection environments, including historical datasets, simulation models, and real systems, enabling practitioners to reduce unnecessary sampling while maintaining a desired level of decision quality.

</details>


### [170] [Smooth, globally Polyak-Łojasiewicz functions are nonlinear least-squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07972)
*Nicolas Boumal,Christopher Criscitiello,Quentin Rebjock*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了黎曼流形上满足Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件的非凸光滑函数的全局结构，揭示了此类函数必然具有平方和形式，其极小值集要么是欧几里得空间（此时函数可化为凸二次型），要么具有奇异几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件在非凸优化中被广泛使用，因为它能保证算法的快速收敛性，但这一局部的一阶不等式对函数全局结构及其极小值集的约束程度尚不清楚。研究旨在揭示PŁ条件对光滑函数全局几何结构的深层影响。

Method: 通过分析黎曼流形上光滑函数满足PŁ条件时的几何特性，证明负梯度流的端点映射构成一个平凡光滑纤维丛，从而推导出函数必然具有非线性平方和形式。利用这一刚性结构，进一步分析极小值集的几何性质。

Result: 1. 在可收缩流形上，满足PŁ条件的光滑函数必然可表示为f(x)=f*+‖φ(x)‖²，其中φ是淹没映射；2. 极小值集要么微分同胚于欧几里得空间（此时函数可通过坐标变换化为凸二次型），要么具有奇异几何（如Whitehead流形）；3. 存在完备黎曼度量使函数保持PŁ条件且成为测地凸函数。

Conclusion: Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件对光滑函数的全局结构施加了强约束：函数必须是平方和形式，其极小值集呈现二重性——要么是简单欧几里得结构，要么是奇异几何。这一发现深化了对非凸优化中PŁ条件几何本质的理解，并为相关算法分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PŁ) condition is often invoked in nonconvex optimization because it allows fast convergence of algorithms beyond strong convexity. A function $f \colon \mathcal{M} \to \mathbb{R}$ on a Riemannian manifold $\mathcal{M}$ is globally PŁ if $\|\nabla f(x)\|^2 \geq 2μ(f(x) - f^*)$ for all $x$, where $f^* = \inf f$ and $μ> 0$. How much does this pointwise, first-order inequality constrain $f$ and its set of minimizers $S$?
  We show that if $f$ is also smooth ($C^\infty$) and $\mathcal{M}$ is contractible (e.g., if $\mathcal{M} = \mathbb{R}^n$), then the PŁ condition imposes a firm global structure: such a function is necessarily of the form $f(x) = f^* + \|\varphi(x)\|^2$ (a nonlinear sum of squares) where $\varphi \colon \mathcal{M} \to \mathbb{R}^k$ is a submersion, and $k$ is the codimension of $S$ in $\mathcal{M}$. The proof hinges on showing that the end-point map of negative gradient flow on $f$ is a trivial smooth fiber bundle over $S$.
  This rigidity leads to a striking dichotomy. Either $S$ is diffeomorphic to a Euclidean space, in which case $f$ can be transformed into a convex quadratic by a smooth change of coordinates. Or $S$ must display genuinely exotic geometry; for example, it can be diffeomorphic to the Whitehead manifold.
  As a further consequence, we show that there exists a complete Riemannian metric on $\mathcal{M}$ under which $f$ remains PŁ and becomes geodesically convex.

</details>


### [171] [Inexact Limited Memory Bundle Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08067)
*Jenni Lampainen,Kaisa Joki,Napsu Karmitsa,Marko M. Mäkelä*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于大规模非光滑非凸优化的新型不精确有限内存束方法，该方法能容忍函数值和次梯度的噪声，并证明了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模非光滑优化问题在实际应用中普遍存在，但获取精确的函数值和次梯度值可能计算成本高昂甚至不可行。许多实际场景中，由于测量误差、建模误差、隐私保护计算或随机近似等原因，只能获得不精确信息，因此需要不精确优化方法。

Method: 提出一种新型不精确有限内存束方法，专门针对大规模非光滑非凸优化问题设计。该方法能够容忍函数值和次梯度中的噪声，采用有限内存技术处理大规模问题。

Result: 证明了所提方法的全局收敛性，能够收敛到近似驻点。数值实验表明，该方法在不同噪声水平下（函数值和/或次梯度值）表现良好，在精确数据和噪声数据下都能有效工作。特别在机器学习差分隐私等噪声不可避免的应用中表现出适用性。

Conclusion: 该方法在大规模非光滑优化中具有竞争力，特别适用于噪声不可避免的实际应用场景，如机器学习中的差分隐私保护计算。

Abstract: Large-scale nonsmooth optimization problems arise in many real-world applications, but obtaining exact function and subgradient values for these problems may be computationally expensive or even infeasible. In many practical settings, only inexact information is available due to measurement or modeling errors, privacy-preserving computations, or stochastic approximations, making inexact optimization methods particularly relevant. In this paper, we propose a novel inexact limited memory bundle method for large-scale nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. The method tolerates noise in both function values and subgradients. We prove the global convergence of the proposed method to an approximate stationary point. Numerical experiments with different levels of noise in function and/or subgradient values show that the method performs well with both exact and noisy data. In particular, the results demonstrate competitiveness in large-scale nonsmooth optimization and highlight the suitability of the method for applications where noise is unavoidable, such as differential privacy in machine learning.

</details>


### [172] [Dual Approaches to Stochastic Control via SPDEs and the Pathwise Hopf Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08155)
*Mathieu Laurière,Jiefei Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文开发了连续时间随机控制问题的对偶方法，用于在高维状态和控制空间中计算鲁棒的对偶界，并证明了广义Hopf公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机控制问题在高维状态和控制空间中面临维度诅咒的挑战，需要开发能够有效计算鲁棒对偶界的新方法。

Method: 基于Rogers的对偶公式，将内层优化问题表述为随机偏微分方程(SPDE)，利用Pontryagin最大值原理和广义Hopf公式构建无维度诅咒的方法。

Result: 在温和条件下证明了广义Hopf公式，数值实验表明对偶方法能有效补充原始方法，包括用于求解高维PDE的深度BSDE方法和强化学习中的深度actor-critic方法。

Conclusion: 提出的对偶方法为高维随机控制问题提供了有效的计算框架，能够与现有原始方法形成互补，具有理论和实际应用价值。

Abstract: We develop dual approaches for continuous-time stochastic control problems, enabling the computation of robust dual bounds in high-dimensional state and control spaces. Building on the dual formulation proposed in [L. C. G. Rogers, SIAM Journal on Control and Optimization, 46 (2007), pp. 1116--1132], we first formulate the inner optimization problem as a stochastic partial differential equation (SPDE); the expectation of its solution yields the dual bound. Curse-of-dimensionality-free methods are proposed based on the Pontryagin maximum principle and the generalized Hopf formula. In the process, we prove the generalized Hopf formula, first introduced as a conjecture in [Y. T. Chow, J. Darbon, S. Osher, and W. Yin, Journal of Computational Physics 387 (2019), pp. 376--409], under mild conditions. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our dual approaches effectively complement primal methods, including the deep BSDE method for solving high-dimensional PDEs and the deep actor-critic method in reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [173] [Free-Energy Minimizing Policies Under Generative Model Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08222)
*Arash Shafiei,Caio César Graciani Rodrigues,Giovanni Russo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于变分自由能的分布鲁棒决策框架，处理生成模型中的模糊性，将其转化为极小极大最优控制问题，并开发具有收敛保证的求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界的决策和控制问题中，生成模型通常存在模糊性和不确定性。传统方法可能无法充分处理这些分布层面的不确定性，需要开发能够在这种模糊性下保持鲁棒性的决策框架。

Method: 提出变分自由能公式，将分布鲁棒决策问题转化为极小极大最优控制问题，其中最大化在表示模糊性的不确定性集合上进行。证明了最优策略的计算需要解决非凸最小化问题，并提出了具有收敛保证的求解算法。

Result: 在摆锤摆起问题上通过仿真验证了所提方法的有效性，展示了算法能够处理生成模型中的模糊性并找到鲁棒的最优策略。

Conclusion: 提出的变分自由能框架为分布鲁棒决策提供了一种理论基础和实用算法，能够有效处理生成模型中的模糊性，并与广泛的学习和控制框架相关联。

Abstract: We present a variational free-energy formulation for distributionally robust decision-making with ambiguity in the generative model. The formulation, related to a broad range of learning and control frameworks, yields a minimax optimal control problem where maximization is over an uncertainty set that represents ambiguities. We prove that computing the optimal policy requires solving a non-convex minimization problem and propose an algorithm with convergence guarantees to find the solution. The effectiveness of our results is illustrated via simulations on a pendulum swing-up problem.

</details>


### [174] [Finite-time Reachability for Constrained, Partially Uncontrolled Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08327)
*Ram Padmanabhan,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种在控制权限部分失效情况下，驱动约束非线性系统在有限时间内到达目标状态的技术


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决约束非线性系统在控制权限部分失效时，如何在有限时间内精确到达目标状态的问题

Method: 基于初始状态构建简单的线性无漂移近似，将有限时间区间划分为逐渐减小的子区间，在每个分区中基于近似动力学设计控制输入

Result: 在时间区间长度有界条件下，证明这些控制输入能使原始非线性系统与目标状态保持有界误差；随着时间分区变短，误差可减小至零，即使存在未受控输入的影响

Conclusion: 该技术能有效处理控制权限部分失效问题，战斗机模型仿真验证了在部分输入失效情况下仍能实现目标状态

Abstract: This paper presents a technique to drive the state of a constrained nonlinear system to a specified target state in finite time, when the system suffers a partial loss in control authority. Our technique builds on a recent method to control constrained nonlinear systems by building a simple, linear driftless approximation at the initial state. We construct a partition of the finite time horizon into successively smaller intervals, and design controlled inputs based on the approximate dynamics in each partition. Under conditions that bound the length of the time horizon, we prove that these inputs result in bounded error from the target state in the original nonlinear system. As successive partitions of the time horizon become shorter, the error reduces to zero despite the effect of uncontrolled inputs. A simulation example on the model of a fighter jet demonstrates that the designed sequence of controlled inputs achieves the target state despite the system suffering a loss of control authority over one of its inputs.

</details>


### [175] [Data Poisoning Attacks Can Systematically Destabilize Data-Driven Control Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08392)
*Vijayanand Digge,Martina Vanelli,Ahmad W. Al-Dabbagh,Julien M. Hendrickx,Gianluca Bianchin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 数据驱动控制中的数据投毒攻击：攻击者可在不了解系统模型的情况下，通过递归数据投毒机制伪造状态轨迹，使任何线性状态反馈控制器失稳物理系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动控制虽然避免了显式模型辨识，但其对数据的依赖引入了新的安全漏洞。本文旨在揭示攻击者如何通过数据投毒攻击破坏控制系统的稳定性，即使攻击者不了解系统模型或控制器合成过程。

Method: 开发了一种递归数据投毒机制，通过生成伪造的状态轨迹，诱导系统动态特性的精确几何偏移。攻击无需了解系统模型或控制器合成过程，仅需在数据层面操作。

Result: 攻击者能够系统性地毒化用于控制合成的数据，导致任何由规划器合成的线性状态反馈控制器使物理系统失稳。数值仿真在无噪声和有噪声数据下均验证了攻击的有效性。

Conclusion: 数据驱动控制管道可以被模型无关的攻击在数据层面确定性地破坏稳定性。这揭示了数据驱动控制范式的安全脆弱性，强调了在控制系统设计中考虑数据安全的重要性。

Abstract: Data-driven control has emerged as a powerful paradigm for synthesizing controllers directly from data, bypassing explicit model identification. However, this reliance on data introduces new and largely unexplored vulnerabilities. In this paper, we show that an attacker can systematically poison the data used for control synthesis, causing any linear state-feedback controller synthesized by the planner to destabilize the physical system. Concerningly, we show that the attacker can achieve this objective without knowledge of the system model or the controller synthesis procedure. To this end, we develop a recursive data-poisoning mechanism that generates falsified state trajectories, inducing a precise geometric shift in the apparent system dynamics. More broadly, our results establish that data-driven control pipelines can be deterministically destabilized by model-agnostic attacks operating solely at the data level. Numerical simulations corroborate these findings for both noise-free and noisy data.

</details>


### [176] [Collective deterrence as a classification problem: Voting rules, deterrence credibility, and escalation risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08482)
*Torgeir Aambø*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究威慑联盟的机构设计选择问题，通过信号模型分析可信威慑与升级风险之间的权衡，为小型联盟构建分类问题来评估不同决策机制的表现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 威慑联盟需要建立机构设计来决定何时对攻击或事件进行报复，这种设计选择涉及可信威慑与升级风险之间的权衡，需要系统研究如何在不同环境下实现最佳平衡

Method: 使用简单的信号模型分析权衡关系，构建相关的二元分类问题，针对四人小型联盟计算各种选择函数和概率分布下的经验ROC曲线统计特征

Result: 通过计算和分析不同选择函数在多种环境下的表现，得出了威慑联盟机构设计的经验ROC曲线统计特征，为评估不同决策机制提供了量化框架

Conclusion: 威慑联盟的机构设计需要在可信威慑与升级风险之间取得平衡，通过信号模型和分类问题分析可以为小型联盟提供有效的决策机制评估方法，帮助选择适合不同环境的机构设计方案

Abstract: Deterrence coalitions that collectively own their deterrence technology, need an institutional design to decide when to retaliate against an attack or incident. This choice of institutional design, formalized through a social choice function, introduces a tradeoff between credible deterrence and escalation risk. We study this tradeoff via a simple signalling model, and use it to construct an associated binary classification problem to determine institutional designs that perform well in a variety of environments. For a small coalition of four members, we compute and study the statistics of the empirical ROC curves associated to a variety of choice functions and probability distributions for retaliation and false positives.

</details>


### [177] [Density-Driven Optimal Control: Convergence Guarantees for Stochastic LTI Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08495)
*Kooktae Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出随机密度驱动最优控制(D²OC)方法，解决多智能体系统的去中心化非均匀区域覆盖问题，通过最小化Wasserstein距离作为运行成本，确保时间平均经验分布收敛到非参数目标密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于密度的方法通常依赖计算量大的欧拉PDE求解器或启发式规划，缺乏严格的收敛保证。需要一种能够连接个体智能体动力学与集体分布匹配的严谨拉格朗日框架。

Method: 提出随机密度驱动最优控制(D²OC)，这是一个随机MPC类问题，将Wasserstein距离最小化作为运行成本。通过可达性分析建立形式化收敛保证，即使在过程和测量噪声存在下也能提供有界跟踪误差。

Result: 数值结果表明，随机D²OC实现了鲁棒的分散覆盖，在最优性和一致性方面优于先前的启发式方法。时间平均经验分布收敛到非参数目标密度，具有有界跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 随机D²OC为多智能体非均匀区域覆盖提供了一个严谨的拉格朗日框架，通过形式化收敛保证和Wasserstein距离优化，在资源受限任务中实现了鲁棒、分散的覆盖性能。

Abstract: This paper addresses the decentralized non-uniform area coverage problem for multi-agent systems, a critical task in missions with high spatial priority and resource constraints. While existing density-based methods often rely on computationally heavy Eulerian PDE solvers or heuristic planning, we propose Stochastic Density-Driven Optimal Control (D$^2$OC). This is a rigorous Lagrangian framework that bridges the gap between individual agent dynamics and collective distribution matching. By formulating a stochastic MPC-like problem that minimizes the Wasserstein distance as a running cost, our approach ensures that the time-averaged empirical distribution converges to a non-parametric target density under stochastic LTI dynamics. A key contribution is the formal convergence guarantee established via reachability analysis, providing a bounded tracking error even in the presence of process and measurement noise. Numerical results verify that Stochastic D$^2$OC achieves robust, decentralized coverage while outperforming previous heuristic methods in optimality and consistency.

</details>
